    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="1" styleName="heading 1">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">第一章  引言</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">自</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">世纪</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">50</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">年代后期集成电路问世以来，固体电子器件的小型化和集成度便在高速、低能耗、和高存储密度的要求下持续迅速地提高。半导体集成电路经过近几十年来的发展，在</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Moore</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">定律“大约每</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">18</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个月芯片的集成度增加一倍”的预言推动下，硅基微电子芯片的特征线宽已经从</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Intel</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">第一代处理器的</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">10μm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">缩小到了</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2011</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">年应用于第三代</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Core</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">处理器的</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">22nm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[1,2]</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，目前正在向</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">14</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">nm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">工艺发展。随着器件的缩小，尺寸限制所带来的量子效应也趋于明显。当器件尺寸达到与电子的费米波长相比拟的长度时，离散能级以及干涉、隧穿等量子效应就会对器件中的电子输运产生决定性的影响。这些小尺度下的新现象和新效应既是对传统半导体器件的挑战，也为开发新型器件提供了机遇。如何突破传统器件的设计思路，利用这些量子效应来实现更高效、低能耗的计算，成为了物理学中的一个研究热点</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[3-5]</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">………</plain_text>

      </text_run>
    </paragraph>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图例和表头的结尾都不推荐有句号，如果实在觉得需要，请保持全文统一，都有句号，或者都没有句号。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="1" styleName="heading 1">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">	</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">第三章  格式详细</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">要求</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文一般应由</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">11</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个主要部分组成，依次为：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">中英文封面，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">版权声明，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">中文摘要，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">4. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文摘要（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">ABSTRACT</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">），</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">5. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">目录，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">6. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">正文（含引言和结论），</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">7. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> 8. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">9. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个人简历、在学期间发表的学术论文与研究成果</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">10.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">致谢、后记或说明，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">11. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文原创性声明和授权使用说明。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文如果有缩略词或者符号表，可以放到目录之后、正文之前（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">5-6</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">之间）。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">以上各部分独立为一部分，每</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">一</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">章节和</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">部分</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">都</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">从</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">新的一页（即奇数页）</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">开始。各部分具体要求如下：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.1  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">封面</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文应采用研究生院指定的统一封面，博士用青绿色封面，同等学力硕士用黄绿色封面，其他硕士用黄色封面。封面上的校徽和“北京大学”字样应采用信息化办公室发布的核准版本。封面上应居中填写：论文题目、姓名、学号、院系、专业、研究方向、导师、完成年月等信息。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">题目应准确概括整个论文的核心内容，简明扼要，一目了然。一般不宜超过</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个汉字（符），采用一号黑体字，居中填写，一行写不完可以分两行填写。如有副标题，在主标题和副标题之间用破折号间隔。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">院系填写培养院系全称，不得使用简称，院系名称前也不写“北京大学”四个字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">专业应使用学位管理系统中标准的专业名称，不得增减字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">导师一栏应填写学籍管理系统中的导师姓名，后衬“教授”、“研究员”等导师职称。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">若指导</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">教师多于一人，则分行署名。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">作者及导师信息部分使用三号仿宋字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">完成论文日期用三号宋体汉字，如“二〇一四年六月”，不用阿拉伯数字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文内封的，紧接中文封面之后，项目内容和中文封面内容一致</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.2  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">版权声明</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">版权声明为全校统一格式、内容。从校内门户或者从研究生院网站下载、打印即可。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.3  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">中文摘要</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">	</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">中文摘要部分的标题为“摘要”，用黑体三号字，居中书写，单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">24</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">18</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">摘要内容用小四号宋体字两端对齐书写，段落首行空两个汉字符，行距为固定值</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">博士中文摘要一般</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">800-1000</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">汉字（符），硕士论文摘要一般</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">600</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">汉字左右。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">内容一般包括：论文研究的目的和意义；完成的工作和方法（作者独立进行的研究工作的概括性叙述）；获得的主要结论或提出的主要观点（这是摘要的中心内容）。硕士学位论文摘要应突出论文的新见解，博士学位论文摘要应突出论文的创新点。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">论文摘要不能出现图片、表格或其他插图材料。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">论文的关键词，是为了文献标引工作从论文中选取出来用以表示全文主题内容信息的单词或术语，应有</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3~5</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个，每个关键词之间用逗号间隔。关键词放</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">摘要页最下方</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，从新的一行撰写。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">如果论文的主体工作得到了有关基金资助，应在摘要第一页的页脚处标注：本研究得到某某基金（编号：□□□）资助。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文培养项目学生使用英文写学位论文，中文摘要应不少于6000字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Students </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">of</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> the </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">English Training Program</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> are </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">required to </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">write their dissertations in English, </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">and</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> their </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Chinese</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> abstracts should be no less than 6,000 </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">C</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">hinese characters.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.4  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文摘要</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文摘要由上到下应包含</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">ABSTRACT</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”、英文摘要内容和关键词（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">KEYWORDS</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">ABSTRACT</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Arial</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">三号字，居中书写，单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">24</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">18</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">摘要内容和关键词（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">KEYWORDS</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）用小四号</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Times New Roman</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">字体书写，两端对齐，标点符号用英文标点符号。固定行距</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段前、段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">KEYWORDS</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”大写，其后的关键词第一个字母大写，关键词之间用半角逗号间隔。关键词放英文摘要页下方，从新的一行写起。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">英文摘要的内容应与中文摘要一致。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.5  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">目录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">目录既是论文的提纲，也是论文组成部分的小标题。目录由章节序号、标题名称和页码组成。章节序号，一般是下级引用上级序号，如</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.2.5</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表示第二章第</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">节第</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">5</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">小节。目录一般列到三级标题，即二级节标题（如</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.2.5</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）即可。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">目录内容一般从第一章引言开始，目录之前的内容及目录本身不列入目录内。目录中的章标题行采用黑体小四号字，固定行距</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">6</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅；其他内容采用宋体小四号字，行距为固定值</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段前、段后均为</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">目录中的章标题行居左书写，一级节标题行缩进</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个汉字符，二级节标题行缩进</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个汉字符。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">论文的图表一般不用专门制作目录，如确有必要，可另起一页放到本目录之后。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.6  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">主要符号对照表</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">	</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">如果论文中使用了大量的符号、标志、缩略词、专门计量单位、自定义名词和术语等，应编写“主要符号对照表”。如果上述符号和缩略词数量不多，可以不设专门的“主要符号对照表”，在论文中出现时随即加以说明即可。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“主要符号对照表”放目录之后、正文之前。格式上“主要符号对照表”同“章”标题，内容同正文格式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.7  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">正文</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">正文是学位论文的主体，根据学科专业特点和选题情况，可以有不同的写作方式。但必须言之成理，论据可靠，严格遵循本学科国际通行的学术规范。内容包括：第一章引言（或绪论、序言、导论等），第二章，……，第□章结论与展望。书写层次要清楚，内容要有逻辑性。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="3" styleName="heading 3">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.7.1  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">标题</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">标题要重点突出，简明扼要，格式如下：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">各章标题，例如：“第一章</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">引言”。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">章序号采用中文数字，章序号与标题之间空一个汉字符，采用黑体三号字，居中书写，单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">24</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">18</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。目录中和章平级的其它标题也用这一格式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">一级节标题，例如：“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.1  </plain_text>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">实验装置与实验方法”。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">节编号用阿拉伯数字表示，前边数字为上级章节的序号，后一数字为本节的顺序号。数字间用半角小数点“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”连接。节标题序号与标题名之间空一个汉字符（下同）。采用黑体四号（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">14pt</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）字居左书写，行距为固定值</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">24</plain_text>
