      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Roman</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">体，两端对齐书写，段落首行左缩进</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个汉字符。行距为固定值</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">20</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅（段落中有数学表达式时，可根据表达需要设置该段的行距），段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="3" styleName="heading 3">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">.7.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">脚注</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">正文中某句话需要具体注释、且注释内容与正文内容关系不大时可以采用脚注方式。在正文中需要注释的句子结尾处用①②③……样式的数字编排序号，以“上标”字体标示在需要注释的句子末尾。在当前页下部书写脚注内容。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">脚注内容采用宋体小五号字，按两端对齐格式书写，单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，段前段</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">后均空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。脚注的序号按页编排，不同页的脚注序号不需要连续。详细规定见本页脚注。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="3" styleName="heading 3">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.7.4  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="13" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">有关图表和表达式</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图、表和表达式按章编号，用两个阿拉伯数字表示，前一数字为章的序号，后一数字为本章内图、表或表达式的顺序号。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">两数字间</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">用半角小数点“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”连接。例如“图</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”，“表</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">5.6</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”，“（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1.2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）”等等。若图或表中有附注，采用英文小写字母顺序编号，附注写在图或表的下方。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图应精选，具有自明性，切忌与表及文字表述重复。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图应清楚，但坐标比例不要过分放大，同一图上不同曲线的点要分别用不同形状的标识符标出。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图中的术语、符号、单位等应与正文表述中所用一致。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图序与</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图名，例如：“图</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.1  1901-2011</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">年西北地区年平均气温分布”。“图</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”是图序，是“第二章第</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个图”的序号，依次类推。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图序与</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图名置于图的下方，采用宋体</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">11pt</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">字居中书写，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">6</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">12</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，行距为单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图序与</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图名文</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">字之间空一个汉字符宽度。图中标注的文字采用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">9~10.5pt</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，以能够清晰阅读为标准。专用名字代号、单位可采用外文表示，坐标轴题名、词组、描述性的词语均须采用中文。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">如果一个图由两个或两个以上分</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图组成</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">时，各分</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
