        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">number</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图和表不推荐连着，建议中间有文字相隔。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表达式</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表达式主要是指数字表达式，例如数学表达式，也包括文字表达式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表达式采用与正文相同的字号居中书写，或另起一段空两个汉字符书写，一旦采用了上述两种格式中的一种，全文都要使用同一种格式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表达式应有序号，序号用括号括起置于表达式右边行末，序号与表达式之间不加任何连线。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">公示</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">居中</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">范例：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <formula tag="formula">x=-b±b2-4ac2a#3.1</formula>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表达式行的行距为单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，段前段后各空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">6</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。当表达式不是独立成行书写时，有表达式的段落的行距为单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，段前段后各空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">公式后，不需要留空行！</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.8  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献是论文中用到的直接引语（数据、公式、理论、观点等）或间接引语以及作者曾经阅读过的相关文献信息资源，是论文的必要组成部分。撰写学位论文时要注意引用权威的和最新的文献。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">著录参考文献必须实事求是，论文中引用过的文献必须著录，未引用的文献不得出现。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献集中著录于正文之后，不得分章节著录。属于外文文献的，直接使用外文著录，不必译成中文。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“参考文献”四个字与章标题格式相同。参考文献表的正文部分用五号字，汉字用宋体，英文用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Times New Roman</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">体，行距采用固定值</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">16</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，标点符号用半角符号。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献的著录方法和文献的标注方式有关，请采用“顺序编码制”。“顺序编码制”是指正文中索引文献时，用顺序编号的方法标注文献。文献序号放“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[]</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”内，以上标方式标注在索引位置。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">以“顺序编码制”索引文献时，其参考文献应按索引对应编号顺序著录。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献的具体著录方法和标注方法见</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.9  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录是与论文内容密切相关、但编入正文会影响整篇论文的条理性和逻辑性的一些资料，是论文主体的补充项目，并不是必须的。以下内容可置于附录之内：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">a.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">放在正文内过分冗长的公式推导；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">b.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">方便他人阅读所需要的辅助性教学工具或表格；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">c.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">重复性数据和图表；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">d.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">非常必要的程序说明和程序全文；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">e.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">关键调查问卷或方案等。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录的格式与正文相同，并依顺序用大写字母</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">B</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">C</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，……编序号，如附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">B</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">C</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，……。只有一个附录时也要编序号，即附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。每个附录应有标题。附录序号与附录标题之间空一个汉字符。例如：“附录</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献著录规则及注意事项”。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">附录中的图、表、数学表达式、参考文献等另行编序号，与正文分开，一律用阿拉伯数字编码，但在数码前冠以附录的序号，例如“图</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A.1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”，“表</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">B.2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”，“式（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">C-3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）”</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">等。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.10  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">致谢、原创性声明和授权使用说明</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文正文和附录之后，一般应放置致谢（后记或说明），主要感谢导师和对论文工作有直接贡献和帮助的人士和单位。致谢言语应谦虚诚恳，实事求是。字数不超过</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1000</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个汉字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">一般致谢的对象有：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（一）指导或协助指导完成论文的导师；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（二）国家科学基金、资助研究工作的奖学金基金、合同单位、资助或支持的企</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">业、组织或个人；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（三）协助完成研究工作和提供便利条件的组织或个人；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（四）在研究工作中提出建议和提供帮助的人；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（五）给予转载和引用权的资料、图片、文献、研究思想和设想的所有者；</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（六）其他应感谢的组织和个人。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文原创性声明和授权使用说明是固定格式、内容，从系统下载、打印放入即可。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">致谢（后记或说明）、学位论文原创性声明和授权使用说明是论文的最后两项内容，目录中和章平级。电子版不签字，装订版要签字。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">如果论文还有其它内容，如个人简历、在学期间发表的学术论文和成果等，可以放附录之后、致谢之前。发表论文列表要求同参考文献列表格式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.11  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页面设置</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">纸张大小：标准</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">A4</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">（</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">21.0cm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">×</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">29.7cm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）尺寸。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页边距：上、下、左、右、装订线的页边距分别为：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.0cm,2.5cm,2.6cm,2.6cm,0cm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，装订线位置：左。左右对称页边距。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页眉和页脚：页眉距边界</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2.0cm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，页脚距边界</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1.75cm</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页眉内容：从“摘要”到最后，每一页均须有页眉。页眉用五号宋体，居中排列，奇偶页不同。奇数页页眉为相应内容的名称、正文中相应各章的名称，偶数页页眉为“北京大学博士学位论文”或“北京大学硕士学位论文”。格式为页眉的文字内容之下划一条横线，线粗</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0.75</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，线长与页面齐宽。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页脚内容：页码。封面和原创声明不要页码，从“摘要”开始至“目录”（或图表目录、主要符号对照表）结束，页码用罗马数字“Ⅰ、Ⅱ、Ⅲ……”表示；从“第一章引言”开始至论文结束，页码用阿拉伯数字“</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">、</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">、</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">……”表示。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页码置于页脚中部，采用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Times New Roman</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">五号字体，数字两侧不加修饰线。经学位办公室批准的英文学位论文，格式要求同上。论文须用中文封面。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="2" styleName="heading 2">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3.12  </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">打印和装订要求</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="14" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文必须打印，不得手写。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">除封面和原创性声明外，一律双面打印。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">3. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">统一用印有我校正确校徽和校名的“硕士研究生学位论文”、“博士研究生学位论文”的封面。封面上各栏目必须认真、正确填写。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">4. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">论文字迹和标点符号清楚、工整、正确，图表清晰、可复印和微缩。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">5. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文一律在左侧装订。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">6. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">封面纸应不低于</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">200</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">克标准。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">7. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">书脊上应印上论文题名（如题名过长，应印成双列）、年份</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">、姓名</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">和学号。论文页数如果不足</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">50</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">书脊需夹垫</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">白纸增厚（加厚至</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">50</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页）装订</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">所谓‘增厚’，是指在书脊处夹放约</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">公分左右的纸条，能显示书脊即可，整本论文是不允许加放空白纸，字数较少的论文，可采取单面打印、缩小书脊字体，或加厚装订等方式打印书脊题名</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">)</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">8. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">涉密学位论文应到保密办公室指定的地点装订，格式同普通论文。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">9</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">书脊上应按顺序印上</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">四</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">项</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">：</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学位论文“题名（如果题名过长，请印成双列）”</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“年份，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">阿拉伯数字加年，如</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2015</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">年</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，无需加月份</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">”</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“姓名”</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“学号”</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="1" styleName="heading 1">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">参考文献</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="8" styleName="heading 8">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">陈</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">浩</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">元</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">著录文后参考文献的规则及注意事项</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[J]. </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">编辑学报</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">, 2005, 12(6): 413-415.</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="8" styleName="heading 8">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">张旭</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">, </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">张通和</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">, </plain_text>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af3" styleName="普通">

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">一页</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">开始新的一部分</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，有页空白没有页码，就出现</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">35页之后就是37页现象，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">为正常</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">现象</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，无需调整。</plain_text>
      </text_run>

      </text_run>

    </paragraph>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">附录需要奇数页另起</plain_text>
      </text_run>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">答辩申请时</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">，请填写下表，单独打印，与</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">本论文一起交至教务办公室，提交论文</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">时</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">，该页请删除。</plain_text>
      </text_run>

      </text_run>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af4" styleName="List Paragraph">

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="1" styleName="heading 1">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">致谢</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">老师作为一名优秀的、经验丰富的教师，具有丰富的</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">xx</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">知识和</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">xx</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">经验，在整个论文实验和论文写作过程中，对我进行了耐心的指导和帮助，提出严格要求，引导我不断开阔思路，为我答疑解惑，鼓励我大胆创新，使我在这一段宝贵的时光中，既增长了知识、开阔了视野、锻炼了心态，又培养了良好的实验习惯和科研精神。在此，我向我的指导老师表示最诚挚的谢意！</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">……</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">)</plain_text>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="黑体" fontSize="16" fontColor="#000000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">北京大学学位论文原创性声明和使用授权说明</plain_text>

    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">本人郑重声明：所呈交的学位论文，是本人在导师的指导下，独立进行研究工作所取得的成果。除文中已经注明引用的内容外，本论文不含任何其他个人或集体已经发表或撰写过的作品或成果。对本文的研究做出重要贡献的个人和集体，均已在文中以明确方式标明。本声明的法律结果由本人承担。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">（必须装订在提交学校图书馆的印刷本）</plain_text>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">本人完全了解北京大学关于收集、保存、使用学位论文的规定，即：</plain_text>
      </text_run>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">按照学校要求提交学位论文的印刷本和电子版本；</plain_text>
      </text_run>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学校有权保存学位论文的印刷本和电子版，并提供目录检索与阅览服务</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="10.5" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">学校可以采用影印、缩印、数字化或其它复制手段保存论文；</plain_text>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">因某种特殊原因需要延迟发布学位论文电子版，授权学校□一年</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">□两年</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

      </text_run>

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">该页可从门户自行打印，无需页眉页脚，奇数页另起。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">答辩后提交的门户论文、纸质版论文以及图书馆论文此页需本人和导师手签，不能用复印件，电子签名、人名章等。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">答辩后提交</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">F</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">TP</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="single">的论文致谢结束即可。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">答辩后提交的门户论文、纸质版论文以及图书馆论文还需增加另外</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">页（均需要奇数页另起），</plain_text>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="true" italic="false" underline="none">按顺序</plain_text>
      </text_run>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">第</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">章用了“顺序编码制索引文献”样式，采用后全文都只能采用这种方式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af5" styleName="脚注">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">图标题在图下方，表标题在表上方。图表序号分章设置，如图</plain_text>

      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">表示第三章第</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">15</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">幅图。</plain_text>
