    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af5" styleName="脚注">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">请用页面</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

      </text_run>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" font="Wingdings" char="F0E0" />
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">奇数</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">页确保</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">每一章节和部分</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">从新的一页开始</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">每一章从新的页面开始即可，无需从</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">新</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">的一页开始</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="12" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">脚注处序号“①，……，⑩”的字体是“正文”，不是“上标”，序号与脚注内容文字之间空半个汉字符，脚注的段落格式为：单</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">倍</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">行距，</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">段前空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0 </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，段后空</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">0 </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">磅，悬挂缩进</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1.5 </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">字符；字号为小五号，汉字用宋体，外文和数字用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Times New Roman </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">字体。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af5" styleName="脚注">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">电子版论文除不用签字外，内容应和纸质版完全一致，应是</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1 </plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">个</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">独立文件，可用</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">word </plain_text>

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">格式或</plain_text>

      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">格式。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="ad" styleName="footnote text">

      </text_run>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">全文参考文献索引方式请选用“顺序编码制”，文献列表也应选择相对应的著录方法。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
    </paragraph>
    <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none" styleId="af5" styleName="脚注">
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
      </text_run>
      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">什么时候需要新开一页，请详见第四章的前</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">部分</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">；每一章</plain_text>

      <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="宋体" fontSize="9" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">从新的一页开始。</plain_text>
      </text_run>
